Q1. A and B work together at first. A's time is 10 days and B's time is 30 days. If A leaves after 2 days, when will the job be finished from the start?
A. 30 days
B. 24 days
C. 21 days
D. 19 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_forward_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W=(r_A+r_B)t_1+r_Bt_2
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=(12+4)\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{88}{4}=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=2+\frac{W-W_1}{B}
\]
Answer: 24 days

---

Q2. If A can complete a work in 30 days and B can complete it in 12 days, how many days are needed when both work at the same time?
A. 21 days
B. 42 days
C. 12 days
D. 8.57 days
Answer: 8.57 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(30,12)=60
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=60/30=2,\quad B=60/12=5
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{60}{2+5}=8.57
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{30\times 12}{30+12}=8.57
\]
Answer: 8.57 days

---

Q3. A team needs 8 working days to finish a job. The job is stopped for 2 days in between. What is the total calendar time?
A. 12 days
B. 11 days
C. 10 days
D. 15 days
Answer: 10 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_interrupted_work
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
T_{calendar}=T_{active}+T_{idle}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
T_{active}=\frac{120}{10+5}
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=T_{active}+2=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_a+t_i
\]
Answer: 10 days

---

Q4. Two filling pipes A and B take 12 and 18 hours respectively. If pipe B is opened 2 hours later, what is the time in hours to fill the tank?
A. 14 hours
B. 8 hours
C. 6 2/5 hours
D. 7 hours
Answer: 8 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: pc_multiple_pipes_timing
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\quad T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=3\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{30}{3+2}=8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Answer: 8 hours

---

Q5. Three typists can complete 12, 15 and 18 pages per hour respectively. If they work together for 8 hours, how many pages will they complete?
A. 366 pages
B. 360 pages
C. 450 pages
D. 361 pages
Answer: 360 pages
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_typist_pages_per_hour
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
O=\sum r_it_i
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
O=12\times 8+15\times 8+18\times 8
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
O=360
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
O=(r_1+r_2+r_3)t
\]
Answer: 360 pages

---

Q6. A and B together complete a work in 10 days, B and C in 12 days, and C and A in 15 days. In how many days will A alone complete it?
A. 24 days
B. 19 1/5 days
C. 28 days
D. 25 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_pairwise_ab_bc_ac
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
2(A+B+C)=(A+B)+(B+C)+(C+A)
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
AB=10,\ BC=12,\ CA=15
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
A=\frac{AB+AC-BC}{2}
\]
Answer: 24 days

---

Q7. A repair team completes 15 metres per day and daily damage removes 3 metres. How long will 120 metres take?
A. 9 days
B. 15 days
C. 14 days
D. 10 days
Answer: 10 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_digging_filling_negative_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
T=\frac{120}{15-3}
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
T=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 10 days

Q8. 60 pages of a 120-page job are complete. The remaining pages are typed at 8 pages per day. In how many days will the job finish?

Put the given values into that relation.
\[
x=\frac{120-60}{8}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
x=7.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
x=\frac{W_l}{r}
\]
Answer: 7 1/2 days

Q9. A can complete a work in 8 days and B can complete it in 18 days. In the same number of days, what is the ratio of the work done by A and B?
A. 18:26
B. 8:18
C. 9:4
D. 26:18
Answer: 9:4
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_work_done_ratio_from_times
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
W_A:W_B=\frac{1}{8}:\frac{1}{18}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
W_A:W_B=18:8
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
W_A:W_B=9:4
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
E=9:4
\]
Answer: 9:4

---

Q10. Water flows into a tank at 15 litres per minute. In 30 minutes, how many litres will enter the tank?
A. 454 litres
B. 447 litres
C. 675 litres
D. 450 litres
Answer: 450 litres
Difficulty: Easy
Family: pc_tank_capacity_from_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
C=rt
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
C=15\times 30
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
C=450
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
C=r\times t
\]
Answer: 450 litres

---

Q12. A, B and C handle documents one day at a time in rotation. Their daily counts are 10, 6 and 3. In how many days will 60 documents be finished?
A. 13.3 days
B. 9.3 days
C. 6.3 days
D. 13.95 days
Answer: 9.3 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_conditional_activation
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=10+6+3
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=9.3
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 9.3 days

---

Q16. A 60-litre tank is filled by alternate filling and draining hours. Filling adds 12 litres and draining removes 5 litres. In how many hours is the tank full?

C. 16 1/3 hours
D. 24 1/2 hours

Family: pc_alternating_spill_cycle

\[
W_{cycle}=12-5
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
60\ units,\ cycle=7
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
T=16\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=C_f+t_f
\]
Answer: 16 1/3 hours

Q18. A printing job produces 80 pages each hour for 8 hours. With 85% acceptance, what is the accepted page count?
A. 540 pages
B. 544 pages
C. 543 pages
D. 652.8 pages
Answer: 544 pages
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_work_quality_rejection
Explanation:
Convert each person's work into contribution units.
\[
O_a=O\times \frac{p}{100}
\]
Use contribution ratio for the share.
\[
O_a=80\times 8\times \frac{85}{100}
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
O_a=544
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
O_a=gross\times p/100
\]
Answer: 544 pages

Q19. A pipe fills a tank in 12 hours. If the tank is already \(\frac{1}{4}\) full, how many hours are needed to fill it completely?

\[

\[
T=\frac{1-1/4}{1/12}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
T=9
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=(1-f)F
\]
Answer: 9 hours

Q24. A and B together can complete a work in 12 days. A alone can complete it in 30 days. In how many days will B alone complete it?
A. 42 days
B. 20 days
C. 18 days
D. 21 days
Answer: 20 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_individual_from_combined
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{12}-\frac{1}{30}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
B=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{12\times 30}{30-12}=20
\]
Answer: 20 days

Q25. A job needs 10 days to finish. After 4 days of work, what part of the job is still left?

B. 3/5
C. 2/5
D. 5/3
Answer: 3/5
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_remaining_work_fraction
Explanation:

\[
R=1-\frac{t}{T}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
R=1-\frac{4}{10}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
R=\frac{6}{10}=3/5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{10-4}{10}=3/5
\]
Answer: 3/5

Q27. Pipe A can fill a tank in 12 hours and pipe B can empty the same tank in 36 hours. If both pipes are opened together, in how many hours will the tank be filled?
A. 7 1/2 hours
B. 10 hours
C. 11 hours
D. 5 hours
Answer: 10 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{15}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 10 hours

Q28. 720 ration packets are available. Daily consumption is 36 packets. How long will the ration last?
A. 21 days
B. 20 days
C. 15 days
D. 10 days
Answer: 20 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_resource_rate_equivalence
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
D=\frac{S}{R}
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
D=\frac{720}{36}
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=\frac{S}{R}
\]
Answer: 20 days

Q34. A filling pipe takes 10 hours for a tank. With a leak, the same tank fills in 20 hours. In how many hours can the leak empty a full tank?

B. 17 hours
C. 25 hours
D. 20 hours
Answer: 20 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{10}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 20 hours

\]

Q38. If A:B working capacity is 10:20, what will be A:B time ratio for one job?
A. 10:20
B. 2:1
C. 20:30
D. 30:20
Answer: 2:1
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_time_ratio_from_efficiency
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
T_A:T_B=E_B:E_A
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
T_A:T_B=20:10
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A:T_B=2:1
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=2:1
\]
Answer: 2:1

\]

Q47. A team processes 15 files per day for the first 3 days. After that, one worker is replaced and the team processes 16 files per day. If 120 files must be completed, how many days are needed in all?
A. 6.15 days
B. 11.69 days
C. 8.69 days
D. 7.69 days
Answer: 7.69 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_replacement_worker
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
r_1t_1+r_2t_2=W
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=15\times 3
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=3+\frac{75}{16}=7.69
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 7.69 days

Q48. A starts a work and B joins after 4 days. A and B can finish the work alone in 18 and 24 days. What is the total time taken?
A. 14 2/5 days
B. 9 days
C. 8 days
D. 12 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\ T_2=\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=20\times 4,\quad R=360-80
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=4+\frac{280}{20+15}=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=4+\frac{360-20\times 4}{20+15}
\]
Answer: 12 days

Q49. Two workers A and B have capacities 3:4. If the job is finished in 12 days by both, how long will A alone take?
A. 28 days
B. 16 days
C. 9 days
D. 19 days
Answer: 28 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_relative_efficiency_percent
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
W=(a+b)T
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
W=(3+4)\times 12
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A=\frac{84}{3}=28
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_A=\frac{(a+b)T}{a}=\frac{(3+4)12}{3}=28
\]
Answer: 28 days

Q50. A and B work on a job but leave before it is completed. A leaves 2 days early and B leaves 3 days early. If their individual times are 10 and 20 days, how long does the job take?
A. 11 1/4 days
B. 15 days
C. 7 days
D. 9 days
Answer: 9 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
18(T-2)+9(T-3)=180
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=9
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 9 days

Q51. A works first and B works next, then the order repeats. They complete 6 and 4 forms on their turns. How many hours are needed for 60 forms?

Answer: 12 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_alternating_hours
Explanation:

\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]

\[
W_{cycle}=6+4
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 12 hours

Q55. A can complete a piece of work in 24 days and B can complete the same work in 30 days. In how many days will they complete it working together?
A. 13 1/3 days
B. 24 days
C. 27 days
D. 54 days
Answer: 13 1/3 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(24,30)=120
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=120/24=5,\quad B=120/30=4
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{120}{5+4}=13\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{24\times 30}{24+30}=13\frac{1}{3}
\]
Answer: 13 1/3 days

Q56. A job is finished by 15 workers in 16 days when each works 6 hours daily. How many workers are needed for 12 days at 8 hours daily?

\[

\]
Answer: 15 workers

D. 16 1/2 days
Answer: 16 1/2 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_food_population_change
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
D=\frac{S-consumed}{people}
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
D=\frac{900-30\times 8}{40}
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D=16.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=\frac{S_l}{P_2}
\]
Answer: 16 1/2 days

\]

\]

Q78. A alone takes 8 days and B alone takes 12 days. A works alone for 4 days, then B joins. How many days will the job take in all?

\[

\[
T=4+\frac{36}{9+6}=6.4

\]

Q85. One worker can do a job in 6 days and another can do it in 15 days. How long will they take together?

Answer: 4.29 days

Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:

\[
W=LCM(6,15)=30
\]

\[

\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{30}{5+2}=4.29
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{6\times 15}{6+15}=4.29
\]
Answer: 4.29 days

Q86. 18 workers can complete a job in 8 days by working 5 hours a day. How many workers are needed to finish it in 12 days by working 6 hours a day?

D. 18 workers

Family: tw_man_days_hours_basic
Explanation:

\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[

\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
M_2=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{18\times 8\times 5}{12\times 6}=10
\]
Answer: 10 workers

\]

\[
M_2=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{10\times 15\times 8}{20\times 6}=10
\]
Answer: 10 workers

Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[

\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=12.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 12 1/2 days

Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(8,24)=24
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=24/8=3,\quad B=24/24=1
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{24}{3+1}=6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{8\times 24}{8+24}=6
\]
Answer: 6 days

Q188. A and B have working capacities in the ratio 4:5. Together they complete a job in 10 days. In how many days will A alone complete it?
A. 12 1/2 days
B. 22 1/2 days
C. 8 days
D. 19 days
Answer: 22 1/2 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_relative_efficiency_percent
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
W=(a+b)T
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
W=(4+5)\times 10
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A=\frac{90}{4}=22.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_A=\frac{(a+b)T}{a}=\frac{(4+5)10}{4}=22.5
\]
Answer: 22 1/2 days

Q189. A and B can separately complete a work in 12 and 24 days. A is absent for the last 2 days and B is absent for the last 3 days. In how many days is the work completed?
A. 10 1/3 days
B. 6 1/3 days
C. 15 1/2 days
D. 11 1/3 days
Answer: 10 1/3 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
10(T-2)+5(T-3)=120
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=10\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 10 1/3 days

Q190. A and B work on alternate hours, starting with A. A completes 6 files in a hour and B completes 4 files in a hour. If 120 files are to be completed, in how many hours will the job be finished?

B. 24 hours
C. 28 4/5 hours
D. 29 hours
Answer: 24 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_alternating_hours
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=6+4
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 24 hours

Q192. The total wage for a job is ₹9000. A, B and C contribute in the ratio 24:36:40. How much should A receive?

B. ₹2160
C. ₹1728
D. ₹2157
Answer: ₹2160
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_wage_distribution_efficiency
Explanation:
Convert each person's work into contribution units.
\[
Share=\frac{c_i}{\sum c}\times W
\]
Use contribution ratio for the share.
\[
Share=\frac{24}{100}\times 9000
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
Share=2160
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[

\]
Answer: ₹2160

Q193. 64 of 180 forms are complete. 8 workers are available and 12 days remain. How many additional workers are required?

B. 4 workers

D. 2.4 workers
Answer: 2 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{116}{1\times 12}\right\rceil-8
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=2
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 2 workers

Q194. One worker can do a job in 12 days and another can do it in 10 days. How long will they take together?
A. 10 days
B. 11 days
C. 5.45 days
D. 22 days
Answer: 5.45 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(12,10)=60
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=60/12=5,\quad B=60/10=6
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{60}{5+6}=5.45
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{12\times 10}{12+10}=5.45
\]
Answer: 5.45 days

Q195. 10 workers can complete a job in 15 days by working 8 hours a day. How many workers are needed to finish it in 20 days by working 6 hours a day?
A. 10 workers
B. 14 workers
C. 15 workers
D. 13 workers
Answer: 10 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_man_days_hours_basic
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
10\times 15\times 8=M_2\times 20\times 6
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
M_2=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{10\times 15\times 8}{20\times 6}=10
\]
Answer: 10 workers

Q196. A job is finished by 12 workers in 10 days when each works 8 hours daily. How many workers are needed for 16 days at 6 hours daily?
A. 19 workers
B. 10 workers
C. 12 workers
D. 13 workers
Answer: 10 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_farm_harvest_workers
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
12\times 10\times 8=M_2\times 16\times 6
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
M_2=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{12\times 10\times 8}{16\times 6}=10
\]
Answer: 10 workers

Q202. Without leakage, a tank fills in 12 hours. With leakage, it fills in 18 hours. Find the leak's emptying time in hours?
A. 35 hours
B. 42 hours
C. 28 4/5 hours
D. 36 hours
Answer: 36 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{12}-\frac{1}{18}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=36
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 36 hours

Q229. A and B work together at first. A's time is 12 days and B's time is 18 days. If A leaves after 4 days, when will the job be finished from the start?

\[

\]

\[
W_1=(15+10)\times 4
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=4+\frac{80}{10}=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=4+\frac{W-W_1}{B}
\]
Answer: 12 days

Q230. A takes 12 days to finish a work and B takes 6 days for the same work. In how many days can A and B finish it together?
A. 6 days
B. 9 days
C. 18 days
D. 4 days

Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:

\[

\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=12/12=1,\quad B=12/6=2
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{12}{1+2}=4
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{12\times 6}{12+6}=4
\]
Answer: 4 days

Q262. A takes 30 days to finish a work and B takes 8 days for the same work. In how many days can A and B finish it together?
A. 38 days
B. 19 days
C. 6.32 days
D. 8 days
Answer: 6.32 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(30,8)=120
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=120/30=4,\quad B=120/8=15
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{120}{4+15}=6.32
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{30\times 8}{30+8}=6.32
\]
Answer: 6.32 days

\[

\]
Answer: 6.71 days

Q306. Two workers take turns, with A starting. A handles 8 files per turn and B handles 5 files per turn. In how many hours will 120 files be finished?
A. 13.78 hours
B. 18.38 hours
C. 19.38 hours
D. 13.38 hours
Answer: 18.38 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_alternating_hours
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=8+5
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=18.38
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 18.38 hours

Q307. 8 workers have completed 48 of 240 forms. Only 10 days are left. How many extra workers are needed to finish on time?
A. 8 workers
B. 18 workers
C. 12 workers
D. 10 workers
Answer: 12 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{192}{1\times 10}\right\rceil-8
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 12 workers

Q308. One worker can do a job in 6 days and another can do it in 12 days. How long will they take together?
A. 4 days
B. 18 days
C. 6 days
D. 9 days
Answer: 4 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(6,12)=12
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=12/6=2,\quad B=12/12=1
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{12}{2+1}=4
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{6\times 12}{6+12}=4
\]
Answer: 4 days

\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=13
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 13 workers

Q341. A and B start a work together. A can complete it in 10 days and B in 20 days. After 3 days, A leaves. In how many days from the start will the work be completed?
A. 14 days
B. 20 days
C. 15 days
D. 17 1/2 days
Answer: 14 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_forward_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W=(r_A+r_B)t_1+r_Bt_2
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=(18+9)\times 3
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=3+\frac{99}{9}=14
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=3+\frac{W-W_1}{B}
\]
Answer: 14 days

Q342. If A can complete a work in 15 days and B can complete it in 30 days, how many days are needed when both work at the same time?
A. 15 days
B. 45 days
C. 22 1/2 days
D. 10 days
Answer: 10 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(15,30)=30
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=30/15=2,\quad B=30/30=1
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{30}{2+1}=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{15\times 30}{15+30}=10
\]
Answer: 10 days

\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{8}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=13\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 13 1/3 hours

---

\]

Q388. A can complete a piece of work in 20 days and B can complete the same work in 26 days. In how many days will they complete it working together?
A. 20 days
B. 46 days
C. 23 days
D. 11.3 days
Answer: 11.3 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(20,26)=260
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=260/20=13,\quad B=260/26=10
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{260}{13+10}=11.3
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{20\times 26}{20+26}=11.3
\]
Answer: 11.3 days

A. 9.29 days
B. 9.74 days
C. 3.74 days
D. 7.74 days
Answer: 7.74 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_replacement_worker
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
r_1t_1+r_2t_2=W
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=24\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{132}{23}=7.74
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 7.74 days

Q411. A can complete a work in 15 days and B can complete it in 21 days. A starts alone, and after 5 days B joins him. In how many days from the start will the whole work be completed?
A. 16 1/4 days
B. 8.83 days
C. 6.83 days
D. 10.83 days
Answer: 10.83 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\ T_2=\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=7\times 5,\quad R=105-35
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=5+\frac{70}{7+5}=10.83
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=5+\frac{105-7\times 5}{7+5}
\]
Answer: 10.83 days

Q412. A and B can separately complete a work in 30 and 12 days. A is absent for the last 2 days and B is absent for the last 3 days. In how many days is the work completed?
A. 16.29 days
B. 13.54 days
C. 11.29 days
D. 12.29 days
Answer: 11.29 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
2(T-2)+5(T-3)=60
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=11.29
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 11.29 days

Q413. A handles 6 files on his turn and B handles 5 files on the next turn. They continue alternately from A. How many hours are needed for 90 files?
A. 16 1/3 hours
B. 22 1/3 hours
C. 13.07 hours
D. 13 1/3 hours
Answer: 16 1/3 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_alternating_hours
Explanation:

\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]

\[
W_{cycle}=6+5
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=16\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 16 1/3 hours

Q414. A job has 300 forms. 8 workers have finished 48 forms, and 10 days remain. How many more workers should be added?
A. 22.5 workers
B. 13 workers
C. 15 workers
D. 18 workers
Answer: 18 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{252}{1\times 10}\right\rceil-8
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=18
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 18 workers

Q415. A can complete a piece of work in 30 days and B can complete the same work in 12 days. In how many days will they complete it working together?
A. 12 days
B. 42 days
C. 21 days
D. 8.57 days
Answer: 8.57 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(30,12)=60
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=60/30=2,\quad B=60/12=5
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{60}{2+5}=8.57
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{30\times 12}{30+12}=8.57
\]
Answer: 8.57 days

Q447. A contract amount is ₹8000. Completion 4 days before the deadline earns ₹200 per day as bonus. What amount is payable?

\[

\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
E=8800
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[

\]

Q448. A alone finishes a job in 12 days, while A and B together finish it in 6 days. How many days will B alone take?
A. 12 days

C. 18 days
D. 6 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_individual_from_combined
Explanation:

\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{12}
\]

\[
B=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{6\times 12}{12-6}=12
\]
Answer: 12 days

Q462. 12 workers have done 96 files out of 360. To finish the rest in 10 days, how many additional workers are needed?

D. 19 workers
Answer: 15 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{264}{1\times 10}\right\rceil-12
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 15 workers

Q463. A takes 30 days to finish a work and B takes 18 days for the same work. In how many days can A and B finish it together?
A. 11 1/4 days
B. 18 days
C. 48 days
D. 24 days
Answer: 11 1/4 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(30,18)=90
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=90/30=3,\quad B=90/18=5
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{90}{3+5}=11.25
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{30\times 18}{30+18}=11.25
\]
Answer: 11 1/4 days

N_{extra}=22

Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 22 workers

\[

\]

Q496. A and B jointly work for 2 days. A then leaves, and B completes the job. If A and B alone need 15 and 8 days, what is the total time?

B. 3.93 days
C. 8 2/3 days
D. 1.93 days
Answer: 6.93 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_forward_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W=(r_A+r_B)t_1+r_Bt_2
\]

\[
W_1=(56+105)\times 2
\]

\[
T=2+\frac{518}{105}=6.93
\]

Q497. A can complete a piece of work in 6 days and B can complete the same work in 15 days. In how many days will they complete it working together?
A. 6 days
B. 21 days
C. 10 1/2 days
D. 4.29 days
Answer: 4.29 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(6,15)=30
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=30/6=5,\quad B=30/15=2
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{30}{5+2}=4.29
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{6\times 15}{6+15}=4.29
\]
Answer: 4.29 days

Q498. A team needs 4 1/2 working days to finish a job. The job is stopped for 1 days in between. What is the total calendar time?

C. 3 1/2 days
D. 5 1/2 days
Answer: 5 1/2 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_interrupted_work
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
T_{calendar}=T_{active}+T_{idle}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
T_{active}=\frac{90}{5+15}
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=T_{active}+1=5.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_a+t_i
\]
Answer: 5 1/2 days

Q499. Construction progresses by 12 metres daily and damage reduces it by 3 metres daily. How many days are required for 150 metres?
A. 16 2/3 days
B. 13 2/3 days
C. 20 days
D. 12 2/3 days
Answer: 16 2/3 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_digging_filling_negative_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
T=\frac{150}{12-3}
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
T=16\frac{2}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 16 2/3 days

\]

\]
